# Progress

One line per psalm, appended by whoever finishes it. `reviewed` = Latinist gate and stylist both ran; `unreviewed` = a critic could not run. Order of work: Sunday Compline (90, 133, 233 = Nunc dimittis), Sunday Prime (53, 117, 118), then Pss 1–150 in order. Ps 4 was held for Gustavo until his delegation (`DECISIONS.md`); it is now at draft 3.
